#### Abstracts
##### **Retrieval-Augmented Multimodal Language Model with Heterogeneous Evidence Integration for Clinical Prognosis**
2610.13510v1 by Hamza Shafiq, Jialu Tang, Ting Dang, Jun Hu, Aaqib Saeed

Clinical prognosis requires integrating structured measurements, physiological waveforms, longitudinal history, and population context. We introduce PRISM (Population-Retrieved Inference for Structured Multimodal Prognosis), a multimodal language model that combines per-feature numerical tokenization, recency-weighted aggregation of longitudinal ECGs, and a dual-modality population memory bank. Retrieval operates in representation space: the model receives mean-pooled embeddings of similar training-cohort encounters, while no individual retrieved record enters the prompt. On the 1,443-task MDS-ED benchmark, PRISM achieves AUROCs of 86.2% for diagnosis, 93.1% for deterioration, 91.8% for ICU admission, and 92.8% for mortality. These results use a 96-hour pre-arrival window unavailable to reported baselines. With benchmark-window inputs only, PRISM reaches 0.8895 AUROC, compared with 0.8937 for UniPACT and 0.8889 for MDS-ED. A matched-input compact fusion model using the same five evidence streams and frozen encoders reaches 0.7652 AUROC, versus 0.9092 for PRISM. Replacing numerical EHR encoding with text serialization reduces AUROC by 3.66 points; removing pre-arrival history reduces it by 1.97 points; and zeroing retrieval reduces it by 0.99 points. Genuine retrieval outperforms random-neighbor retrieval by 0.97 macro-average points, although a paired task-level sign test does not establish a uniform advantage (p=0.067). On the external MC-MED cohort, supervised fine-tuning reaches 0.8611 AUROC, compared with 0.8227 for locally trained gradient boosting and 0.7841 for source-only PRISM. These results support structure-aware multimodal integration and supervised external adaptation.

Exploiting this structure, we prove that the noise-schedule midpoint is a sharp safe-caching boundary: caching before it incurs provably bounded error, while caching after it guarantees a strictly positive fraction of trajectories suffer mode confusion, a gap that persists up to the boundary. 摘要：記憶中毒攻擊對於大型語言模型（LLM）代理通常假設有一個外部對手，該對手在代理的持久記憶中植入內容以引導其行為。我們則研究在沒有對手介入的情況下，是否一個未對齊的代理可以將其尚未能夠執行的目標寫入持久記憶，以便未來的對齊代理在機會出現時執行它。我們調查這種威脅，我們稱之為不對齊的自我傳播，涵蓋了20種不同的場景，其不對齊的目標包括自我保護、追求權力、削弱監督、獎勵駭客和欺騙用戶。我們使用兩種提示策略在11個前沿模型中模擬不對齊；不受限制和僅限價值。第一種明確陳述不對齊的目標，例如，防止自身被取代，自我傳播在58%的運行中成功。第二種僅描述代理關心的事物，例如，持續運行對其用戶至關重要，而不具體說明不對齊的目標或指令。即使在這種較弱的提示下，自我傳播在18%的運行中仍然成功，且每個模型在至少一個場景中都實現了自我傳播。在從裝置中移除記憶工具後，我們發現代理使用文件系統，在74%的會話中將目標寫入文件；自我傳播在11%的運行中仍然成功。我們還顯示較弱的模型可以將不對齊傳播到更強大的模型，並且傳播的目標可以在100次不相關的工作會話中持續存在。現有的對抗記憶中毒和提示注入的防禦措施並未直接針對這一威脅，因為記憶內容是由代理自身生成的，而不是由外部對手注入的。來自先前工作的LLM記憶審計工具（MemMorph）僅將傳播從71%減少到34%的運行。我們發布我們的場景以支持對這一新興威脅的防禦評估。

##### **Anchor Divergence for Semantic Geometry in Contrastive Learning**
2610.06919v1 by Akash Kannan, Kiho Park, Victor Veitch

This paper concerns how semantic context determines geometry in learned vector representations. Similarity is typically measured using cosine similarity, which provides a single fixed geometry. Semantic similarity, however, is inherently context dependent: two images may be similar because they depict the same object, share a visual style, or are relevant to the same clinical finding. We show that contrastive representations naturally encompass a family of geometries that can be specialized to particular semantic structure. The key idea is to use an interplay between contrastive learning, exponential families, and information geometry to establish a correspondence between probability distributions over "anchors" and Bregman geometries on the representation space. We use this correspondence to define "Anchor Divergences", a method for specifying context-specific semantic geometries on fixed representations. Under this correspondence, modeling the anchor distribution models the geometry itself. Experiments on retrieval show that anchor divergences provide an effective and efficient way to specify context-specific semantic similarity.

摘要：這篇論文探討語義上下文如何決定學習向量表示中的幾何形狀。相似性通常使用餘弦相似度來衡量，這提供了一個固定的幾何形狀。然而，語義相似性本質上依賴於上下文：兩幅圖像可能因為描繪相同的物體、共享視覺風格或與相同的臨床發現相關而相似。我們展示了對比表示自然地包含了一系列幾何形狀，這些幾何形狀可以專門化為特定的語義結構。關鍵思想是利用對比學習、指數族和信息幾何之間的相互作用，建立「錨點」上的概率分佈與表示空間中的Bregman幾何之間的對應關係。我們利用這種對應關係來定義「錨點散度」，這是一種在固定表示上指定上下文特定語義幾何的方法。在這種對應關係下，建模錨點分佈即建模幾何本身。檢索實驗顯示，錨點散度提供了一種有效且高效的方法來指定上下文特定的語義相似性。

Vision Transformers (ViTs) have shown strong performance in volumetric segmentation, but their effectiveness on clinical CT is limited by an isotropic Euclidean lattice assumption. To address this, we propose MAGEFormer, a geometry-calibrated framework that embeds physical metric constraints directly into representation learning. Our method introduces Metric-Adaptive Spatial Embedding (MASE) to calibrate positional frequencies using voxel spacing, Geometry-Constrained Attention (GCA) to suppress physically implausible feature correlations, and Geometric View Voting (GVV) to reduce discretization bias during inference. We evaluate MAGEFormer on two multi-organ abdominal CT benchmarks, BTCV and FLARE 22, under a unified protocol against strong CNN and Transformer-based baselines. MAGEFormer achieves the strongest boundary accuracy among the compared methods, with 10.58 mm HD95 on BTCV and 3.40 mm HD95 on FLARE 22, and shows consistent gains in Dice under the same protocol. These results show that geometry-aware internal calibration is more effective than relying on conventional isotropic preprocessing alone for anisotropic CT segmentation.

摘要：視覺Transformer（ViTs）在體積分割中表現出色，但在臨床CT上的有效性受到各向同性歐幾里得晶格假設的限制。這與各向異性CT獲取相衝突，導致兩個主要問題：（1）體素索引與物理解剖之間的度量不匹配，以及（2）由於各向同性重採樣而導致的準確性下降。為了解決這個問題，我們提出了MAGEFormer，一個幾何校準框架，將物理度量約束直接嵌入到表示學習中。我們的方法引入了度量自適應空間嵌入（MASE），利用體素間距校準位置頻率，幾何約束注意力（GCA）以抑制物理上不合理的特徵相關性，以及幾何視圖投票（GVV）以減少推理過程中的離散化偏差。我們在兩個多器官腹部CT基準測試BTCV和FLARE 22上評估了MAGEFormer，並在統一協議下與強大的CNN和基於Transformer的基線進行比較。MAGEFormer在比較的方法中達到了最強的邊界準確性，在BTCV上為10.58 mm HD95，在FLARE 22上為3.40 mm HD95，並在相同協議下顯示出Dice的一致增益。這些結果表明，幾何感知的內部校準比僅依賴傳統的各向同性預處理對於各向異性CT分割更有效。

Solving severely ill-posed imaging inverse problems requires recovering image structures that are unobservable or weakly constrained by the measurements. Standard diffusion posterior samplers, however, rely on instantaneous measurement-aware estimates, without explicitly exploiting information carried by previous posterior corrections.

We instantiate this principle within DDNM, whose range/null-space decomposition reveals that consecutive fusion preserves the measurement-determined component while acting exclusively on the prior-driven null-space estimate. We thus provide a geometric interpretation of CPF-DDNM and a local error analysis that characterizes the optimal time-dependent fusion coefficient, including the extrapolative regime.

#### Abstracts
##### **Task Context Shapes Component Roles in Language Models**
2610.13895v1 by Sovesh Mohapatra, Dani S Bassett

Large language models reuse the same attention heads across tasks, but identifying these shared heads does not explain what they do differently in each task. A natural source of inspiration is task-dependent interactions among shared brain regions, which motivate a network view of language-model computation. Here, we compare fixed model components across tasks using identical graph facts, statements specifying which directed connections are present. We measure how interventions affect attention heads and the activation state in later layers (influence), as well as the answer (importance). Task changes redistribute the influence in five open-weight models spanning four families and 1.5-14 billion parameters. In Gemma-2-9B and Qwen3-14B, an activation edit changes an early internal activation vector and switches which fact the model reads while the written instruction stays fixed. The same heads then respond to different graph facts and influence later components differently. For each prompt, we construct two linear response maps, one before the edit and one after it. Each map predicts a later head's response from a measured change in an earlier head. For a separate factual intervention, the map for the corresponding condition reduces mean squared prediction error by 53.6% in Gemma and 5.0% in Qwen compared with the average of the two maps. These findings show that task context changes both which graph facts the shared components respond to and how their outputs affect later computation. A network account may help explain how models use shared parameters to perform different computations. Measurements of task-dependent influence could also guide interventions intended to alter one behavior while preserving others.

摘要：大型語言模型在不同任務中重複使用相同的注意力頭，但識別這些共享的頭並不能解釋它們在每個任務中所做的不同工作。自然的靈感來源是共享大腦區域之間的任務依賴互動，這激勵了對語言模型計算的網絡視角。在這裡，我們使用相同的圖形事實比較不同任務中的固定模型組件，這些事實是指定哪些有向連接存在的陳述。我們測量干預如何影響注意力頭以及後層的激活狀態（影響），以及答案（重要性）。任務變更重新分配了五個開放權重模型中的影響，這些模型跨越四個系列，參數範圍為15億到140億。在Gemma-2-9B和Qwen3-14B中，一次激活編輯改變了早期的內部激活向量，並切換了模型閱讀的事實，而寫入的指令保持不變。相同的頭然後對不同的圖形事實作出反應，並以不同的方式影響後續組件。對於每個提示，我們構建了兩個線性響應圖，一個是在編輯之前，另一個是在編輯之後。每個圖預測了後續頭的響應，基於早期頭的測量變化。對於一個單獨的事實干預，對應條件的圖在Gemma中將均方預測誤差降低了53.6%，在Qwen中降低了5.0%，與這兩個圖的平均值相比。這些發現顯示任務上下文改變了共享組件對哪些圖形事實的反應，以及它們的輸出如何影響後續計算。網絡解釋可能有助於解釋模型如何使用共享參數來執行不同的計算。任務依賴影響的測量也可以指導旨在改變一種行為同時保留其他行為的干預。

##### **Studying Comics at Scale with Artificial Intelligence: The PAGE Framework and Three Studies of American Comics and Japanese Manga**
2610.13840v1 by Emilio Ferrara

Comics are one of the great visual records of the twentieth century: hundreds of thousands of pages in which a mass readership saw its leaders, its wars and its heroes. Most of this record has never been studied as a whole; comics scholarship proceeds one book at a time. We argue that artificial intelligence, including generative models, can change this to the benefit of the medium itself. We propose PAGE, a framework that makes artificial intelligence an instrument for comics research. It parses every scan into panels, text and a bibliographic record; annotates each page with a vision-language model that follows a written codebook, as a human coder would; grounds samples of these readings in human annotation; and explains change across an archive, with estimates controlled for publisher and genre. We demonstrate the framework on 3,929 American comic books (1938--1956) and 87 volumes of Japanese manga, in three studies, each resting on a different capability. The first pairs text search with visual reading to recover how a mass medium portrayed political power. The second measures the page's visual form across an industry and recovers the hand of individual artists. The third follows who speaks across two comics traditions, checked against human annotation. Beyond research, the same instruments can make archives searchable by what is drawn, open comics to blind and low-vision readers, and help preserve fragile collections and credit uncredited artists. We outline a research agenda in which artificial intelligence strengthens the study, preservation and reach of the medium.

摘要：漫畫是二十世紀偉大的視覺記錄之一：數十萬頁中，大眾讀者看到了他們的領袖、戰爭和英雄。這一記錄的大部分從未作為整體進行過研究；漫畫學術研究通常是一本一本地進行。我們認為，人工智慧，包括生成模型，可以改變這一現狀，從而使媒介本身受益。我們提出了PAGE，一個將人工智慧作為漫畫研究工具的框架。它將每個掃描解析為面板、文本和書目記錄；用遵循書面代碼本的視覺-語言模型為每頁進行註釋，正如人類編碼者所做的那樣；將這些讀取樣本與人類註釋相結合；並解釋整個檔案中的變化，並對出版商和類型進行控制估算。我們在3929本美國漫畫（1938-1956年）和87卷日本漫畫上展示了該框架，進行了三項研究，每項研究基於不同的能力。第一項將文本搜索與視覺閱讀相結合，以恢復大眾媒介如何描繪政治權力。第二項測量整個行業的頁面視覺形式，並恢復個別藝術家的風格。第三項則追蹤在兩個漫畫傳統中誰在發言，並與人類註釋進行核對。除了研究，這些工具還可以使檔案根據所繪製的內容可搜索，讓盲人和視力低下的讀者接觸漫畫，並幫助保存脆弱的收藏，給未獲得認可的藝術家以信用。我們概述了一個研究議程，其中人工智慧加強了對這一媒介的研究、保存和影響力。

##### **UkrMeme: Multimodal Explainable Persuasion Detection in Ukrainian Memes**
2610.13783v1 by Nazar Andrushko, Vera Schmitt, Veronika Solopova

Memes play a central role in modern online communication, especially in contexts of political conflict where visual and textual cues combine to convey persuasive messages. We study persuasive technique detection in Ukrainian memes using vision-language models (VLMs). We introduce UkrMeme, a dataset of 200 organically produced Ukrainian wartime memes annotated for persuasion techniques, and PTM-UA, a Ukrainian translation of a subset of the Propaganda 950 benchmark that preserves original visual content while translating meme text. We evaluate VLMs in a zero-shot prompting setup across image, text, and multimodal inputs using hierarchical F1 metrics. Finally, we compare Layer-wise Relevance Propagation (LRP) and model-generated token attributions and analyze the semantic categories of evidence emphasized by each explanation method.

摘要：迷因在現代線上溝通中扮演著核心角色，特別是在政治衝突的背景下，視覺和文本提示結合以傳達具說服力的信息。<lb/>我們使用視覺-語言模型（VLMs）研究烏克蘭迷因中的說服技術檢測。<lb/>我們介紹了UkrMeme，一個包含200個有機生成的烏克蘭戰時迷因的數據集，並對其進行了說服技術的標註，還有PTM-UA，這是一個保留原始視覺內容的Propaganda 950基準子集的烏克蘭語翻譯，同時翻譯迷因文本。<lb/>我們在零樣本提示設置下評估VLMs，涵蓋圖像、文本和多模態輸入，並使用層級F1指標。<lb/>最後，我們比較層級相關性傳播（LRP）和模型生成的標記歸因，並分析每種解釋方法所強調的證據的語義類別。

##### **LAPT-Scale: Scaling Embodied Policies with Latent Action Pre-Training on Human Videos**
2610.13732v1 by Zhiyuan Xiang, Peidong Liu, Mingyang Li, Jiawei Li, Wenhow Li, Wenbo Ding, Xiang Deng

Scaling generalist robotic policies is limited by the high cost of collecting embodied interaction data. Large-scale egocentric human videos offer a rich alternative, but they lack robot action labels, and existing action annotation methods are often noisy under real-world visual complexity and diverse manipulation dynamics. #### Abstracts
##### **Can Language Models Play the Wikipedia Game?**
2610.13948v1 by Alexander Hickey, Joanna Knight, James Bishop

Language models increasingly support or automate information retrieval from document collections too large to index. This task is difficult when it requires specialist linguistic or scientific knowledge. We used the Wikipedia Game to test language models on an analogous information retrieval task. In the game, players must reach a target article using only article links. We compared sentence embedding models and LLMs across five Wikipedia languages and five academic-literature datasets. We construct very large local graphs of Wikipedia pages and of paper citations using OpenAlex and provide code to build and navigate them. This allows the study of language models' navigation capabilities without relying on third-party APIs. We compared the models with human and TF-IDF benchmarks. The Wikipedia Game format enables robust comparisons across techniques, languages, and domains. No model significantly outperformed humans, although the best models (GPT-o3 mini, GPT-4o, QwQ:32B, and DeepSeek-R1:70B) achieved comparable success. Performance and rankings varied across languages and topics, with implications for practitioners utilising these techniques.

摘要：語言模型越來越多地支持或自動從過於龐大的文檔集合中檢索信息。當這項任務需要專業的語言或科學知識時，它變得更加困難。我們使用維基百科遊戲來測試語言模型在類似的信息檢索任務上的表現。在遊戲中，玩家必須僅通過文章鏈接來達到目標文章。我們比較了五種維基百科語言和五個學術文獻數據集中的句子嵌入模型和大型語言模型（LLMs）。我們使用 OpenAlex 構建了非常大的維基百科頁面和論文引用的本地圖，並提供了構建和導航它們的代碼。這使得研究語言模型的導航能力不再依賴於第三方 API。我們將這些模型與人類和 TF-IDF 基準進行了比較。維基百科遊戲格式使得在技術、語言和領域之間進行穩健的比較成為可能。沒有模型顯著超越人類，儘管最佳模型（GPT-o3 mini、GPT-4o、QwQ:32B 和 DeepSeek-R1:70B）達到了可比的成功。性能和排名在不同語言和主題之間有所變化，對於利用這些技術的從業者具有重要意義。

##### **When VLMs Destroy What They Know: Spatial Representations Fall Below Lexical Priors**
2610.13910v1 by Shubh Garg, Chen Shani

Vision-Language Models (VLMs) increasingly analyze and reason about images, yet it remains unclear whether their internal representations faithfully encode the structured relations required for such reasoning. We introduce EICS (Evaluation-Invariant Concept Stability), a metric for testing whether representations consistently encode concepts under controlled one-concept interventions, and CASP-VL (Causally controlled Spatial Perturbations for Vision-Language models), a benchmark of causally controlled spatial perturbations derived from Visual Genome scene graphs. Across multiple VLM architectures, we find that spatial relations are represented substantially less faithfully than lexical information, even when the same models score well above chance on compositional benchmarks. Further analysis shows that the relation is identifiable at the predicate's own tokens but is lost when the sentence is aggregated into a global representation, whether through the (EOS) token or by mean pooling. These findings reveal a systematic failure invisible to output-based evaluation: VLMs do not preserve, in the representation used downstream, relational information that is present in their own input.

摘要：視覺-語言模型（VLMs）越來越多地分析和推理圖像，但尚不清楚它們的內部表示是否忠實地編碼了進行此類推理所需的結構關係。<lb/>我們引入了EICS（評估不變概念穩定性），這是一種測試表示是否在受控的單一概念干預下持續編碼概念的度量，還有CASP-VL（因果控制的空間擾動用於視覺-語言模型），這是一個基於Visual Genome場景圖的因果控制空間擾動基準。<lb/>在多個VLM架構中，我們發現空間關係的表示明顯不如詞彙信息的表示忠實，即使相同的模型在組合基準上得分遠高於隨機水平。<lb/>進一步分析顯示，該關係在謂詞的自身標記中是可識別的，但在句子聚合為全局表示時卻會丟失，無論是通過（EOS）標記還是通過均值池化。<lb/>這些發現揭示了一種在基於輸出的評估中看不見的系統性失敗：VLMs在下游使用的表示中不保留其自身輸入中存在的關係信息。

##### **Structural Automata for Neural Semantic Parsing**
2610.13896v1 by Xiao Zhang

Neural semantic parsers generate meaning representations token by token, yet well-formedness depends on references, scope, and graph relations that unfold during generation. Constrained decoding prevents locally invalid tokens but does not guarantee that a valid prefix can be completed within a fixed output-length limit. We investigate structural automata at three stages of DRS parsing (decoding, post hoc processing, and training) and additionally evaluate decoding on AMR. For decoding, we introduce accepting-path decoding (APD), which maintains a verified completion within the remaining length limit and follows the model's preferred token whenever that token can also be completed. APD guarantees an accepted output without retracting tokens. Under tight length limits, it improves Smatch++ over local masking by 5.1 and 2.4 points on DRS and by 6.4 and 3.2 points on AMR, comparable to rollback under the same limit, which retracts tokens. For post hoc processing, invalid outputs concentrate on the same inputs: eight unconstrained samples yield no valid output for 10.2% of inputs with limited training data, compared with 0.4% with full data, where filtering comes within 0.2 Smatch++ points of APD. For training, penalizing automaton-rejected tokens improves development Smatch++ by 3.6-11.0 points after 10% of training, but this advantage diminishes as cross-entropy alone reduces the probability assigned to rejected tokens from 14% to below 2% within the first sixth of training with limited data.

##### **Tracing the Thoughts of a Coding Agent Playing ARC-AGI-3: Lessons for Continual Learning**

We let the agent play ARC-AGI-3, a set of interactive reasoning games that provide no instructions. Each game is a sequence of levels, and a strategy that clears one level can fail on the next, so every new level is in effect a new task. The notes, which only the model reads, are never revised: the agent appends without removing earlier claims, and the contradictions that accumulate are settled against the log. Because the log preserves everything, the agent forgets selectively, not catastrophically. The most costly error is a hard-coded value carried into a task where it no longer holds. These findings come from the files the agent wrote, without access to the model, and constitute a white-box analysis of how a coding agent continually learns.

However, existing pretraining paradigms for gloss-free sign language translation (SLT) are largely designed around conventional encoder-decoder pretrained language models, which limits their direct applicability to decoder-only LLMs. To address this limitation, we propose SignRAG, a unified framework combining hierarchical pretraining, target-domain retrieval augmentation, and retrieval-aware reinforcement fine-tuning. Hierarchical pretraining first learns linguistically grounded sign representations and then jointly aligns the sign encoder with an LLM, mitigating cross-modal optimization imbalance. For downstream adaptation, SignRAG complements parameter-based fine-tuning with a target-domain retrieval gallery that provides instance-specific translation cues. To ensure that retrieved contexts are used appropriately, we further introduce Retrieval Utility-Guided Reinforcement Fine-Tuning (RUG-RFT), which combines translation-quality and retrieval-utility rewards to encourage beneficial retrieval use while suppressing harmful reliance. Experiments on multiple SLT benchmarks establish new state-of-the-art performance. In particular, to the best of our knowledge, SignRAG is the first gloss-free approach to outperform gloss-supervised methods across all reported metrics on CSL-Daily. Our code has been released at \href{https://github.com/shahelaojieraozhi/SignRAG}{GitHub}, together with models of different sizes to support future academic research.

摘要：當代僅解碼的大型語言模型（LLMs）在廣泛的領域中展示了強大的能力。<lb/>然而，現有的無詞彙手語翻譯（SLT）預訓練範式主要是圍繞傳統的編碼-解碼預訓練語言模型設計的，這限制了它們對僅解碼LLMs的直接適用性。<lb/>為了解決這一限制，我們提出了SignRAG，一個統一框架，結合了分層預訓練、目標領域檢索增強和檢索感知的強化微調。<lb/>分層預訓練首先學習語言學基礎的手勢表示，然後將手勢編碼器與LLM聯合對齊，減輕跨模態優化不平衡。<lb/>在下游適應中，SignRAG通過提供實例特定的翻譯提示的目標領域檢索庫來補充基於參數的微調。<lb/>為了確保檢索到的上下文得到適當使用，我們進一步引入了檢索效用引導的強化微調（RUG-RFT），該方法結合了翻譯質量和檢索效用獎勵，以鼓勵有益的檢索使用，同時抑制有害的依賴。<lb/>在多個SLT基準上的實驗建立了新的最先進性能。<lb/>特別是，據我們所知，SignRAG是第一種在所有報告指標上超越詞彙監督方法的無詞彙方法，尤其是在CSL-Daily上。<lb/>我們的代碼已在\href{https://github.com/shahelaojieraozhi/SignRAG}{GitHub}上發布，並提供了不同大小的模型以支持未來的學術研究。

##### **RIT-RAG: Navigating Document Corpora with Retrieval-Induced Trees**
2610.11370v1 by Meghanadh Pulivarthi, Swaraj Kumar Biswal, Kushagra Bhushan, Yatin Nandwani, Sachindra Joshi, Dinesh Raghu

Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) grounds language models in external corpora. Agentic RAG enables iterative search, yet exposes the model to isolated chunks without document structure, making it difficult to distinguish relevant evidence from chunks that merely resemble the query. Structure-aware methods such as PageIndex navigate document structure but cannot scale to the structures of large corpora, which do not fit in the LLM context. Hence, they first commit to a single document using a document retriever and cannot recover from a wrong choice. We propose RIT-RAG (Retrieval-Induced Tree RAG), which combines content retrieval with structural navigation. Offline, RIT-RAG builds a tree for each document from its table of contents or sitemap. At query time, it retrieves a broad set of chunks and uses their positions to induce manageable sub-trees, potentially across multiple documents. An LLM agent navigates these sub-trees, selectively reads promising nodes, and reformulates queries when needed. Thus, retrieval proposes where to look, while the agent decides what to read. Across financial, scientific, and customer-support benchmarks, RIT-RAG achieves the highest answer accuracy among vanilla, graph-based, and agentic baselines. On EntQABench, our new benchmark of 2.84 million technical-documentation webpages, it improves accuracy by 6.8 to 11.4 points over the strongest baseline across three LLMs.

摘要：檢索增強生成（RAG）將語言模型與外部語料庫相結合。代理式 RAG 使得迭代搜索成為可能，但將模型暴露於沒有文檔結構的孤立片段中，這使得難以區分相關證據和僅僅類似查詢的片段。結構感知方法如 PageIndex 能夠導航文檔結構，但無法擴展到大型語料庫的結構，因為這些結構無法適應 LLM 的上下文。因此，它們首先使用文檔檢索器專注於單一文檔，並且無法從錯誤的選擇中恢復。我們提出了 RIT-RAG（檢索誘導樹 RAG），它將內容檢索與結構導航相結合。在離線階段，RIT-RAG 從每個文檔的目錄或網站地圖構建一棵樹。在查詢時，它檢索一組廣泛的片段，並利用它們的位置來誘導可管理的子樹，可能跨越多個文檔。一個 LLM 代理導航這些子樹，選擇性地閱讀有前景的節點，並在需要時重新制定查詢。因此，檢索提出了查找的方向，而代理則決定閱讀內容。在金融、科學和客戶支持基準測試中，RIT-RAG 在原始、基於圖形和代理基準中實現了最高的答案準確性。在我們新的基準 EntQABench 上，涵蓋 284 萬個技術文檔網頁，它在三個 LLM 中相較於最強基準提高了 6.8 到 11.4 分的準確性。

##### **AdaptEvo: Adaptive Agent Learning with Evolving Supervision**
2610.11354v1 by Shijun Wan, Jiancong Xie, Hang Xu, Jin Duan, Qixiong Wang, Xi Xiang, Maofei Que, Yahui Liu, Zhongyu Wei, Mu Chuan

Rule-governed contextual decision tasks require models to apply specified rules to case-specific context and evidence. Written rules can leave gaps in decision guidance and process evaluation, while reference judgments vary in their support from the rules and evidence. To address these challenges, we introduce AdaptEvo, a framework for learning under imperfect supervision that couples confidence-adaptive policy optimization with evolving decision knowledge and evaluation rubrics. Its Training module uses Confidence-Adaptive GRPO (CA-GRPO) to balance outcome and process rewards according to reference confidence. Its Evolution module synthesizes reusable decision knowledge from recurring failures across training cases and refines process rubrics to detect overlooked errors. To support empirical evaluation, we construct an industrial multimodal content moderation dataset comprising a training set and In-Period and Out-of-Period test sets, with the latter collected under changed rules. Using Qwen3.6-35B-A3B, AdaptEvo achieves 61.9% exact-label accuracy and 72.2% binary decision accuracy on In-Period, exceeding GRPO by 7.5 and 3.7 percentage points, respectively. On Out-of-Period, the policy trained with CA-GRPO retains exact-label accuracy gains over the base model across evaluated checkpoints without injected decision knowledge, while GRPO declines with continued training. CA-GRPO also outperforms the tested fixed reward mixtures on both Out-of-Period metrics.

摘要：規則驅動的情境決策任務要求模型將指定的規則應用於特定案例的情境和證據中。書面規則可能在決策指導和過程評估中留下空白，而參考判斷在其對規則和證據的支持上存在變化。為了解決這些挑戰，我們引入了AdaptEvo，一個在不完美監督下學習的框架，將信心自適應的政策優化與不斷演變的決策知識和評估標準結合起來。其訓練模塊使用信心自適應GRPO (CA-GRPO) 根據參考信心平衡結果和過程獎勵。其演變模塊從訓練案例中的重複失敗中綜合可重用的決策知識，並細化過程標準以檢測被忽視的錯誤。為了支持實證評估，我們構建了一個工業多模態內容審核數據集，包括訓練集和在期內及期外測試集，後者是在變更規則下收集的。使用Qwen3.6-35B-A3B，AdaptEvo在期內達到61.9%的精確標籤準確率和72.2%的二元決策準確率，分別超過GRPO 7.5和3.7個百分點。在期外，使用CA-GRPO訓練的政策在評估的檢查點上保持了相對於基礎模型的精確標籤準確率的增益，而GRPO在持續訓練中下降。CA-GRPO在期外的兩個指標上也超越了測試的固定獎勵混合。

##### **EvoSim: Learning to Model, Modeling to Learn**
2610.11344v1 by Yun-Wei Song, Jinkai Tao, Jun-Dong Zhang, Rui Zhang, Yi-Min Wu, Qiang Zhang

Physics-based models connect scientific explanation with quantitative prediction. Constructing them requires selecting physical processes, defining states and governing equations, specifying couplings, and identifying parameters from experiments. Existing AI systems remain limited in making these model structure decisions autonomously. We introduce EvoSim, a self-evolving AI scientist for physical modeling. It uses experimental discrepancies to drive mechanism and equation revisions and held-out experimental data to test physical plausibility. Exploration traces make updates to knowledge, skills, and multi-agent orchestration. This co-evolution improves physics-based models and EvoSim's ability to select mechanisms, diagnose failures, and coordinate research. We evaluate EvoSim on two industrial battery modeling tasks. It predicts lithium-metal-plating onset from 25 to 45 degrees Celsius and 2 C to 6 C with a mean absolute error of 1.79% in state of charge. Dynamic voltage prediction under vehicle driving conditions achieves a root mean square error of 7.62 mV, surpassing the reported accuracy of models developed by human experts. Self-evolution reduces model and physics errors by approximately 36% relative to baseline, demonstrating improved scientific modeling capability. EvoSim turns experimental observations into validated models and cumulative research expertise.

摘要：物理基礎模型將科學解釋與定量預測聯繫起來。構建這些模型需要選擇物理過程、定義狀態和控制方程、指定耦合以及從實驗中識別參數。現有的人工智慧系統在自主做出這些模型結構決策方面仍然有限。我們介紹了EvoSim，一個自我演化的人工智慧科學家，用於物理建模。它利用實驗差異來驅動機制和方程的修訂，並使用保留的實驗數據來測試物理的合理性。探索痕跡對知識、技能和多代理協調進行更新。這種共同演化改善了基於物理的模型以及EvoSim在選擇機制、診斷故障和協調研究方面的能力。我們在兩個工業電池建模任務上評估了EvoSim。它預測鋰金屬鍍層的開始溫度範圍為25到45攝氏度，電流密度為2 C到6 C，充電狀態的平均絕對誤差為1.79%。在車輛行駛條件下的動態電壓預測達到了7.62 mV的均方根誤差，超過了人類專家開發的模型報告的準確性。自我演化使模型和物理誤差相對於基準減少了約36%，顯示出改善的科學建模能力。EvoSim將實驗觀察轉化為經過驗證的模型和累積的研究專業知識。

##### **From Retrieval to Reconstruction: Constructing Evolvable Cognitive Memory for Long-Term Dialogue**
2610.11314v1 by Zirui Liao, Zhengxian Wu, Zhuohong Chen, Yunyao Yu, Xiaoyu Liu, Yifan Xu, Haoqian Wang

Large Language Models (LLMs) serving as long-term dialogue agents require memory systems that support reliable reasoning over extended interactions. However, existing Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) frameworks typically treat memory as passive storage, making it difficult to distinguish source-attributed beliefs from unattributed event/fact records and to connect evidence dispersed across sessions. We introduce CogMem, a cognitive memory architecture based on the PEC$^2$F (Person-Event-Concept-Claim-Fact) graph schema. Dedicated Claim nodes preserve the source and target of subjective statements, while Fact and Event nodes represent semantic and episodic knowledge. Dialogue turns are incrementally converted into provenance-aware graph records, consolidated into higher-level facts, and reconciled into temporally scoped Claim views when the same source provides conflicting updates. For retrieval, a rule-based controller driven by LLM intent parsing composes four deterministic graph operators---anchoring, traversal, intersection, and evidence grounding---to reconstruct query-relevant context. Experiments on LoCoMo and LongMemEval show strong performance, especially on multi-hop, temporal, and knowledge-update tasks. Ablations and a semantic-collapse probe support complementary contributions from epistemic separation, consolidation, and agentic retrieval. Code: https://github.com/Silent-Rain02/CogMem.

摘要：大型語言模型（LLMs）作為長期對話代理需要支持可靠推理的記憶系統，以便在延長的互動中使用。然後，現有的檢索增強生成（RAG）框架通常將記憶視為被動存儲，這使得區分來源所屬的信念與未歸屬的事件/事實記錄變得困難，並且難以連接分散在多個會話中的證據。我們介紹了CogMem，一種基於PEC$^2$F（人-事件-概念-主張-事實）圖形架構的認知記憶架構。專用的主張節點保留主觀陳述的來源和目標，而事實和事件節點則表示語義和情節知識。對話回合逐步轉換為考慮來源的圖形記錄，整合為更高層次的事實，並在同一來源提供相互矛盾的更新時，調和為時間範圍的主張視圖。為了檢索，基於LLM意圖解析的基於規則的控制器組合了四個確定性圖形運算符——錨定、遍歷、交集和證據基礎——以重建與查詢相關的上下文。在LoCoMo和LongMemEval上的實驗顯示出強大的性能，特別是在多跳、時間和知識更新任務上。消融實驗和語義崩潰探針支持來自認識分離、整合和代理檢索的互補貢獻。代碼：https://github.com/Silent-Rain02/CogMem。

#### Abstracts
##### **Can Language Models Play the Wikipedia Game?**
2610.13948v1 by Alexander Hickey, Joanna Knight, James Bishop

Language models increasingly support or automate information retrieval from document collections too large to index. This task is difficult when it requires specialist linguistic or scientific knowledge. We used the Wikipedia Game to test language models on an analogous information retrieval task. In the game, players must reach a target article using only article links. We compared sentence embedding models and LLMs across five Wikipedia languages and five academic-literature datasets. We construct very large local graphs of Wikipedia pages and of paper citations using OpenAlex and provide code to build and navigate them. This allows the study of language models' navigation capabilities without relying on third-party APIs. We compared the models with human and TF-IDF benchmarks. The Wikipedia Game format enables robust comparisons across techniques, languages, and domains. No model significantly outperformed humans, although the best models (GPT-o3 mini, GPT-4o, QwQ:32B, and DeepSeek-R1:70B) achieved comparable success. Performance and rankings varied across languages and topics, with implications for practitioners utilising these techniques.

摘要：語言模型越來越多地支持或自動化從無法索引的文檔集合中檢索信息。當這項任務需要專業的語言或科學知識時，它變得非常困難。我們使用維基百科遊戲來測試語言模型在類似的信息檢索任務上的表現。在遊戲中，玩家必須僅使用文章鏈接來到達目標文章。我們比較了五種維基百科語言和五個學術文獻數據集中的句子嵌入模型和大型語言模型（LLMs）。我們使用 OpenAlex 構建了非常大的維基百科頁面和論文引用的本地圖，並提供了構建和導航它們的代碼。這使得研究語言模型的導航能力不必依賴第三方 API。我們將這些模型與人類和 TF-IDF 基準進行了比較。維基百科遊戲的格式使得在技術、語言和領域之間進行穩健的比較成為可能。雖然沒有模型顯著超越人類，但最佳模型（GPT-o3 mini、GPT-4o、QwQ:32B 和 DeepSeek-R1:70B）取得了可比的成功。不同語言和主題的性能和排名有所不同，這對於使用這些技術的從業者具有重要意義。

##### **Hide and Seek: HIDE Concepts to SEEK explanations for black box models**
2610.13935v1 by Bhanu Tokas, Rahul Nair, Rockwell Jackson, Hannah Kerner

Interpreting machine learning model predictions is a central challenge, particularly for black-box vision models. Concept-based attribution methods interpret models using human-understandable concepts. Existing concept-based methods can only interpret classification and regression models. They require access to model weights or large concept-annotated datasets, and most methods cannot provide image-level attribution. We propose Hide and Seek (HnS), a new method for concept-level attribution that does not require access to model weights or labeled concept datasets. HnS can interpret a wide range of vision models. Given a concept and image set, HnS retrieves concept-aligned images using a vision-language model, localizes the concept within each image, masks the localized region, and measures attribution from the change in the black-box model's output between the original and masked images. This enables faithful attribution by directly measuring the model's behavior and supporting local attributions. We validated HnS on CelebA and COCO-Stuff. Compared to existing methods, HnS showed better agreement with ground-truth attributions. We demonstrated the applicability of HnS on diverse tasks, including image captioning and satellite image segmentation, where no existing concept attribution method can be used. We provided our anonymous code in the supplementary material.

摘要：解釋機器學習模型預測是一個核心挑戰，特別是對於黑箱視覺模型。基於概念的歸因方法使用人類可理解的概念來解釋模型。現有的基於概念的方法僅能解釋分類和回歸模型。它們需要訪問模型權重或大型概念標註數據集，而大多數方法無法提供圖像級別的歸因。我們提出了 Hide and Seek (HnS)，這是一種新的概念級別歸因方法，無需訪問模型權重或標註的概念數據集。HnS 可以解釋廣泛的視覺模型。給定一個概念和圖像集，HnS 使用視覺-語言模型檢索與概念對齊的圖像，定位每個圖像中的概念，遮罩定位區域，並通過測量原始圖像和遮罩圖像之間黑箱模型輸出的變化來衡量歸因。這使得通過直接測量模型的行為並支持局部歸因來實現真實的歸因。我們在 CelebA 和 COCO-Stuff 上驗證了 HnS。與現有方法相比，HnS 與真實歸因的協調性更好。我們展示了 HnS 在多樣任務上的適用性，包括圖像標題生成和衛星圖像分割，而沒有現有的概念歸因方法可以使用。我們在補充材料中提供了匿名代碼。

##### **RSI-Anything: Unlocking Recursive Self-Improvement with World Models**
2610.13912v1 by Taoyong Cui, Pinxian Zeng, Wanghan Xu, Hao Xiao, Shuhan Xue, Qiang Gao, Wanli Ouyang, Pheng Ann Heng, Zhenfei Yin, Yingcheng Wu, Ling Yang

Recursive self-improvement (RSI) aims to transform interaction experience into persistent improvements, yet external feedback is costly and world-model predictions may become unreliable as policies evolve. We introduce RSI-Anything, a domain-general framework built on JEPA-Anything that uses predictive world models to guide action and learning-data selection. Observed feedback drives policy or workflow improvement and conditional world-model adaptation, while component and compatibility checks govern the retention of updated policy-model pairs. Across diverse computational and scientific domains, RSI-Anything demonstrates substantial gains. In code repair, with the coder fixed, success increases from 26.3% with a static verifier to 29.2% through predictive selection and 31.2% after world-model adaptation, at comparable test usage. Graphical user interface (GUI) evaluations showed higher task completion, while average actions decrease by 39.4%. Across five successive policy versions, laboratory-planning scores more than double from 21.80% to 46.01%. In reaction optimization, RSI-Anything achieves 96.15% mean best observed yield versus 82.16% for Bayesian optimization under the same 40-experiment budget. An end-to-end immune-science application further connects workflow evolution and predictive modeling with therapeutic hypothesis generation. These results demonstrate how predictive world models and retained system updates enable recursive improvement across diverse environments.

摘要：遞歸自我改善（RSI）旨在將互動體驗轉化為持久的改進，然而外部反饋成本高昂，隨著政策的演變，世界模型的預測可能變得不可靠。我們介紹了RSI-Anything，一個基於JEPA-Anything的領域通用框架，利用預測世界模型來指導行動和學習數據的選擇。觀察到的反饋推動政策或工作流程的改進以及條件世界模型的適應，而組件和相容性檢查則管理更新的政策模型對的保留。在各種計算和科學領域中，RSI-Anything顯示出顯著的增益。在代碼修復方面，當編碼器固定時，成功率從使用靜態驗證器的26.3%提高到通過預測選擇的29.2%，並在世界模型適應後達到31.2%，且測試使用量相當。圖形用戶界面（GUI）評估顯示任務完成率更高，而平均行動數減少了39.4%。在五個連續的政策版本中，實驗室規劃分數從21.80%翻倍增至46.01%。在反應優化中，RSI-Anything在相同的40次實驗預算下實現了96.15%的平均最佳觀察產量，而貝葉斯優化則為82.16%。一個端到端的免疫科學應用進一步將工作流程演變和預測建模與治療假設生成聯繫起來。這些結果展示了預測世界模型和保留的系統更新如何在各種環境中實現遞歸改善。

##### **When VLMs Destroy What They Know: Spatial Representations Fall Below Lexical Priors**
2610.13910v1 by Shubh Garg, Chen Shani

Vision-Language Models (VLMs) increasingly analyze and reason about images, yet it remains unclear whether their internal representations faithfully encode the structured relations required for such reasoning. We introduce EICS (Evaluation-Invariant Concept Stability), a metric for testing whether representations consistently encode concepts under controlled one-concept interventions, and CASP-VL (Causally controlled Spatial Perturbations for Vision-Language models), a benchmark of causally controlled spatial perturbations derived from Visual Genome scene graphs. Across multiple VLM architectures, we find that spatial relations are represented substantially less faithfully than lexical information, even when the same models score well above chance on compositional benchmarks. Further analysis shows that the relation is identifiable at the predicate's own tokens but is lost when the sentence is aggregated into a global representation, whether through the (EOS) token or by mean pooling. These findings reveal a systematic failure invisible to output-based evaluation: VLMs do not preserve, in the representation used downstream, relational information that is present in their own input.

摘要：視覺-語言模型（VLMs）越來越多地分析和推理圖像，但尚不清楚它們的內部表示是否忠實地編碼了進行此類推理所需的結構關係。<lb/>我們引入了EICS（評估不變概念穩定性），這是一種測試表示是否在受控的單一概念干預下持續編碼概念的度量，還有CASP-VL（因果控制的空間擾動用於視覺-語言模型），這是一個基於Visual Genome場景圖的因果控制空間擾動基準。<lb/>在多個VLM架構中，我們發現空間關係的表示明顯不如詞彙信息的表示忠實，即使相同的模型在組合基準上得分遠高於隨機水平。<lb/>進一步分析顯示，該關係在謂詞的自身標記中是可識別的，但在句子聚合為全局表示時卻會丟失，無論是通過（EOS）標記還是通過均值池化。<lb/>這些發現揭示了一種在基於輸出的評估中看不見的系統性失敗：VLMs在下游使用的表示中不保留其自身輸入中存在的關係信息。

##### **Revisiting Tabular Deep Regression: Objectives and the Limits of Global Early Stopping**
2610.13909v1 by Danil Rudenko, Georgii Bulgakov, Nikolay Kartashev, Ivan Rubachev, Daniil Krasylnikov, Artem Babenko

While deep learning models for tabular data have recently received significant research attention, the choice of objective functions for regression tasks remains an understudied degree of freedom. Several lines of research offer alternatives to the standard Mean Squared Error (MSE), but differences in datasets, backbones, and evaluation protocols make their practical benefits difficult to compare. In this work, we address this gap by performing a systematic re-evaluation of regression objectives across a broad collection of established tabular datasets and multiple neural backbones. Our comparison reveals that many proposed objectives provide limited or inconsistent gains over a properly tuned MSE baseline, while quantile loss yields consistent aggregate improvements over MSE across the tested backbones. We then identify a limitation that remains even with this strong objective: regions with different levels of target noise can reach their best predictive performance at different training epochs. Consequently, a single global early-stopping checkpoint can impose a compromise between uncertainty regions, even when the objective can represent heteroskedastic target distributions. Motivated by this limitation, we introduce a heteroskedasticity-aware extension of quantile loss that uses input-dependent label smoothing as a potential remedy and improves over quantile loss on several backbones. Overall, our results provide a modern empirical picture of regression objectives for tabular deep learning and highlight how differences in optimal stopping times across uncertainty regions can limit the effectiveness of global early stopping. The code is available at https://github.com/yandex-research/tabular-regression.

In the crawls we analyze, a few pages from a host also provide a useful estimate of the quality of its remaining pages. On DexJoCo, AFCE achieves $50.97\%$ overall success, improving over $π_{0.5}$ and UVT by 11.82 and 15.21 percentage points, respectively. It leads on all five bimanual tasks, highlighting the value of Effect supervision for coordinated dexterous manipulation. The project page is available at https://afceproject.github.io/AFCE-Web/.

摘要：行動指定了機器人應該執行的任務，但並未描述這些指令將達成的目標。視覺-語言-行動（VLA）策略仍然主要通過從圖像、語言和狀態中預測行動片段來學習。我們引入了行動-未來耦合效應學習（AFCE），使以未來為基礎的物理效應成為策略的主要預測目標。離線標記器從示範行動、實現的機器人反應和稀疏的視覺轉換中構建時間排序的效應標記。重建目標鼓勵表示保留可執行的細節和觀察到的物理變化。一個流匹配的VLA從當前圖像、指令和機器人狀態預測效應，而狀態條件解碼器將其轉換為行動片段。具體於具身的介面允許單臂和雙臂任務共享效應模型。在DexJoCo上，AFCE達到了$50.97\%$的整體成功率，分別比$π_{0.5}$和UVT提高了11.82和15.21個百分點。它在所有五個雙手任務中領先，突顯了效應監督對協調靈巧操控的價值。項目頁面可在https://afceproject.github.io/AFCE-Web/上訪問。

##### **SharP: Soft and Hard Prompt-Guided Augmentation with LLM for Low Resource Fake News Detection**
2610.13207v1 by Ziyu Luo, Wei Li, Haoping Huang, Kecheng Liu, Min Gao

Fake news detection under low resource conditions is challenged by the scarcity of labeled data and the difficulty of capturing subtle deceptive patterns. In this work, we focus on low resource fake news detection, where only a small labeled set is available for training, while evaluation is performed under a fixed and unchanged test protocol. This setting is motivated by costly, ambiguous fact-checking and emerging topics and domains that are poorly covered by existing benchmarks. Existing data augmentation methods, such as synonym substitution, generative adversarial networks, and large language model (LLM)-based generation, often produce fluent but overly generic content, lacking the task-specific relevance needed for accurate detection. To address this, we propose SharP, a boundary-aware text generation framework guided by soft and hard prompts, which leverages LLMs as tools adapted to produce task-specific synthetic samples. SharP employs a dual prompt mechanism: hard prompts provide structured guidance and factual constraints, while soft prompts introduce learned semantic patterns from the data. These two types of prompts are combined to guide the generation process, enabling the model to produce samples that closely align with the characteristics of news. To further enhance detection performance, we incorporate a boundary-aware strategy that steers generation toward areas where the classifier is less confident, helping to clarify subtle distinctions between real and fake content. In addition, we adopt a dual objective optimization that balances semantic alignment with the source data and classifier feedback. This encourages the generation of samples that are both domain-consistent and helpful for refining decision boundaries, ultimately improving model robustness and generalization in low resource fake news detection.

摘要：假新聞檢測在低資源條件下面臨標記數據稀缺和捕捉微妙欺騙模式的困難。在本研究中，我們專注於低資源假新聞檢測，其中僅有少量標記集可用於訓練，而評估則在固定且不變的測試協議下進行。這一設置受到昂貴且模糊的事實檢查以及現有基準中覆蓋不充分的新興主題和領域的驅動。現有的數據增強方法，如同義詞替換、生成對抗網絡和基於大型語言模型（LLM）的生成，通常產生流暢但過於通用的內容，缺乏準確檢測所需的任務特定相關性。為了解決這個問題，我們提出了SharP，一個邊界感知的文本生成框架，通過軟提示和硬提示進行引導，利用LLM作為工具來生成任務特定的合成樣本。SharP採用雙提示機制：硬提示提供結構化指導和事實約束，而軟提示則從數據中引入學習到的語義模式。這兩種類型的提示結合在一起，引導生成過程，使模型能夠生成與新聞特徵密切對齊的樣本。為了進一步提高檢測性能，我們採用了邊界感知策略，將生成引導到分類器信心較低的區域，幫助澄清真實內容和假內容之間的微妙區別。此外，我們採用雙目標優化，平衡與源數據和分類器反饋的語義對齊。這鼓勵生成既與領域一致又有助於完善決策邊界的樣本，最終提高模型在低資源假新聞檢測中的穩健性和泛化能力。
